Bash, up, down, tests; then verify fast and the final check from Git Bash). The U3 driver, shim, jq wrapper and section 3b
script were taken from the tester's scratch copies (the ones copied at the end of the U3 file). Differences from U3, on purpose:
  - own project COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u4win and own image tag KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4win-464fa11 (fresh images: built in phase

    recount with sbom_check.unlicensed_report on the written files); the syft cache warning counted and one occurrence shown;

    libpq pin read with its sed lines, its pin regex, `docker pull "$base_ref"`, `docker save`, the base SBOM through

    read-only mounts); differences from offline_check.sh: the drift work dir is in <scratch>, not the repo's dist/, and the
    pull's stdout is shown, not sent to /dev/null. Two tester negative controls on the same docker run (wrong libpq pin; the
    ke-app SBOM as its own base): both exit 1 naming the problem. The base was already in the image store (untagged, pulled
    by an earlier offline_check run) and was not removed (a shared image), so the pull is the warm no-op case ("Image is up to
    date"); a cold pull was not exercised here;
  - NEW section 3c (tester diagnostic, no repo change): why syft cannot create /tmp/cache: the syft image's own /tmp is
    `drwxr-xr-x 0/0`, the tmpfs takes that mode, so uid 197610 gets "permission denied"; syft with `--tmpfs /tmp:mode=1777`
    prints no cache warning. Supports reviewer U4 B1;
  - bundle.sh `docker tag`s ke-app/ke-gateway:bundle-464fa1165504 (which existed before, from the developer's run) to the
    tester's images; section 3b put both back on their earlier image IDs (69aa296b..., 32c37421...) and the final check shows

    a documenter item from U3;

    has image ID a4a898bebde2 (U3's 2becf293 was removed in U3 section 8) with the same Created time as U3's; app.Dockerfile is

#### SECTION 3c (Git Bash, tester diagnostic, after 3b and before phase up) ##########################################

==================== 3c. Diagnostic (tester; no repo change): why syft cannot create /tmp/cache as the caller's uid:gid ====================

The compose tmpfs entry '/tmp' is HostConfig.Tmpfs {"/tmp": ""}, the same as docker run --tmpfs /tmp. The syft image has no shell, so the
probe runs the ke-app image (sh, stat) with the sbom service's shape: --read-only, --tmpfs /tmp, --user $(id -u):$(id -g), no network.

$ docker inspect --format "{{json .HostConfig.Tmpfs}}" $(KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER=1:1 docker compose -p ke-tester-u4win-diag --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml create sbom 2>/dev/null >/dev/null; docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win-diag) ; KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER=1:1 docker compose -p ke-tester-u4win-diag --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml rm -f sbom >/dev/null 2>&1; docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win-diag | wc -l  # the tmpfs options compose gives the sbom container (created, never started, then removed); then the count left

See 'docker inspect --help' for more information

# U4 (PSC-22, ruling 5 item 6, ruling 6) Windows proof driver (tester). The U3 driver (docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u3/windows-run.txt) with project, tag, marker and hash list changed
# (docker/source-policy.json added), section 2b extended again (XDG_CACHE_HOME resolves to /tmp/cache on the tmpfs); sections 4b and 4c kept. Section 3b (bundle.sh) runs from Git Bash between phases build and up.
# Own project and image tag so no other project or image is touched.
param([Parameter(Mandatory)][ValidateSet('pre', 'build', 'up', 'down', 'tests')][string]$Phase)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
$PSStyle.OutputRendering = 'PlainText'
$repo = 'C:\repos\Knowledge-engine'
$scratch = Split-Path -Parent $PSCommandPath
$shimDir = Join-Path $scratch 'shim'
$envBak = Join-Path $scratch 'env.bak'
$project = 'ke-tester-u4win'
$tag = 'u4win-464fa11'
Set-Location $repo
$env:COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME = $project
$env:KE_IMAGE_TAG = $tag
$filter = "label=com.docker.compose.project=$project"

function Section([string]$t) { ''; "==================== $t ===================="; '' }
function Step([string]$label, [scriptblock]$sb) {
    "PS> $label"
    $global:LASTEXITCODE = $null
    & $sb 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { "$_" }
    "[exit code: $global:LASTEXITCODE]"
    ''
}
function EnvDiff {
    Compare-Object (Get-Content .env.example) (Get-Content .env) |
        ForEach-Object { '{0} {1}' -f $(if ($_.SideIndicator -eq '=>') { '+ .env        ' } else { '- .env.example' }), $_.InputObject }
}
function ProjectPorts {
    docker ps --filter $filter --format '{{.Names}}  {{.Ports}}'
}

$pyImpl = 'import psycopg; print(psycopg.pq.__impl__, psycopg.pq.version())'
$pyMaps = 'import importlib.util as u, psycopg; psycopg.pq.version(); print("psycopg_c.pq:", u.find_spec("psycopg_c.pq").origin); print("libpq mapped:", sorted({l.split()[-1] for l in open("/proc/self/maps") if "libpq" in l})); print("psycopg_binary spec:", u.find_spec("psycopg_binary"))'
$pyPip = "import importlib.util as u; print(u.find_spec('pip'))"
$shLdd = 'for f in /opt/venv/lib/python3*/site-packages/psycopg_c/pq*.so; do echo "$f"; ldd "$f" | grep -E "libpq|psycopg_binary"; done'

        "user:         $($s.user)"
        "network_mode: $($s.network_mode)"
        "read_only:    $($s.read_only)"
        "cap_drop:     $(@($s.cap_drop) | ConvertTo-Json -Compress)"
        "security_opt: $(@($s.security_opt) | ConvertTo-Json -Compress)"
        "tmpfs:        $(@($s.tmpfs) | ConvertTo-Json -Compress)"
        "services.sbom keys: $(($keys | Sort-Object) -join ', ')"
        "absent (must all be True): " + ((@('ports', 'profiles', 'networks', 'privileged', 'cap_add', 'devices', 'pid', 'ipc') | ForEach-Object { "$_=$(-not ($keys -contains $_))" }) -join ' ')
        'volumes:'
        foreach ($v in $s.volumes) { "  - $($v | ConvertTo-Json -Compress)" }
        "environment:  $($s.environment | ConvertTo-Json -Compress)"
        "XDG_CACHE_HOME (NEW, U4): '$($s.environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME)'; equals '/tmp/cache' (ordinal): $([string]::Equals([string]$s.environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME, '/tmp/cache', [StringComparison]::Ordinal)); under a tmpfs entry: $(@(@($s.tmpfs) | Where-Object { ([string]$s.environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME).StartsWith(([string]$_).TrimEnd('/') + '/') }).Count -gt 0); TMPDIR: '$($s.environment.TMPDIR)'"
        "environment keys: $(@($s.environment.PSObject.Properties.Name).Count) ($((@($s.environment.PSObject.Properties.Name) | Sort-Object) -join ', '))"
        "top-level keys: $((@(($j | ConvertFrom-Json).PSObject.Properties.Name) | Sort-Object) -join ', ')"
        $global:LASTEXITCODE = $rc
    }
    Step 'docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom (must equal the pin)' {
        $ref = docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom
        $rc = $LASTEXITCODE
        "config --images sbom: $ref"
        "pin:                  $pin"
        "equal (ordinal): $([string]::Equals(($ref -join "`n"), $pin, [StringComparison]::Ordinal))"
        $global:LASTEXITCODE = $rc
    }
    $env:KE_SBOM_IN = '/nonexistent'
    $env:KE_SBOM_OUT = '/nonexistent'
    Step "same with the bundle.sh dummy form KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER=$($dummyUser): config --images sbom; resolved user and volumes" {
        docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom
        $j = (docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --format json) -join "`n"
        $rc = $LASTEXITCODE
        $s = ($j | ConvertFrom-Json).services.sbom
        "  user: $($s.user)"
        "  XDG_CACHE_HOME: $($s.environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME); tmpfs: $(@($s.tmpfs) -join ',')"
        foreach ($v in $s.volumes) { "  - $($v | ConvertTo-Json -Compress)" }
        $global:LASTEXITCODE = $rc
    }
    $env:KE_SBOM_IN = $dummyIn
    $env:KE_SBOM_OUT = $dummyOut
    Remove-Item Env:KE_SBOM_USER
    Step 'fail-closed (NEW, U3): KE_SBOM_USER unset (KE_SBOM_IN, KE_SBOM_OUT set): docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config (must fail naming KE_SBOM_USER)' { docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config }
    $env:KE_SBOM_USER = $dummyUser
    Remove-Item Env:KE_SBOM_IN
    Step 'fail-closed (U2, repeated): KE_SBOM_IN unset (KE_SBOM_OUT, KE_SBOM_USER set): docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config (must fail naming KE_SBOM_IN)' { docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config }
    Remove-Item Env:KE_SBOM_OUT, Env:KE_SBOM_USER
    Step 'KE_SBOM_* removed from the process environment (names left)' { @(Get-ChildItem env: | Where-Object { $_.Name -like 'KE_SBOM_*' } | ForEach-Object Name).Count }
    Step 'after: dummy bind sources still absent; anchore/syft images unchanged (nothing pulled); no sbom container created' {
        "Test-Path $dummyIn = $(Test-Path $dummyIn); Test-Path $dummyOut = $(Test-Path $dummyOut); Test-Path C:\ke-tester-u4win-sbom-dummy = $(Test-Path C:\ke-tester-u4win-sbom-dummy)"
        docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' anchore/syft
        'sbom containers: ' + @(docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.service=sbom).Count
        "compose projects named $project*: " + ((docker compose ls -a --filter "name=$project" --format json | ConvertFrom-Json) | ForEach-Object Name)
    }
}

    Section '3. scripts\build.ps1'
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\build.ps1' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\build.ps1 }
    Step "docker image ls --filter reference=ke-*:$tag" { docker image ls --filter "reference=ke-*:$tag" }
}
'up' {
    Section '4. scripts\launch.ps1'
    Step 'diff .env.example .env' { EnvDiff }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 }
    Step 'docker compose ps' { docker compose ps }
    Step 'docker compose ps --format json (service/state/health)' { docker compose ps --format json | ForEach-Object { $o = $_ | ConvertFrom-Json; '{0,-10} {1,-8} {2}' -f $o.Service, $o.State, $o.Health } }
    Step 'Invoke-WebRequest http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz' { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz; "$($r.StatusCode) $($r.Content)" }
    Step 'curl.exe -sS -i http://127.0.0.1:47100/readyz' { curl.exe -sS -i http://127.0.0.1:47100/readyz }
    Step 'Invoke-WebRequest http://127.0.0.1:47101/ (Phoenix UI title)' { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing http://127.0.0.1:47101/; "$($r.StatusCode) " + ([regex]::Match($r.Content, '<title>[^<]*</title>').Value) }
    Step "docker port $project-gateway-1" { docker port "$project-gateway-1" }
    Step 'project containers and published ports' { ProjectPorts }
    Step 'Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 47100,47101 (host listeners)' { Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 47100, 47101 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { '{0}:{1}' -f $_.LocalAddress, $_.LocalPort } | Sort-Object -Unique }
    Step 'TCP connect <this host non-loopback IPv4, not recorded>:47100 within 3 s (must be False)' {
        $ip = (Get-NetIPAddress -AddressFamily IPv4 | Where-Object { $_.IPAddress -notlike '127.*' -and $_.IPAddress -notlike '169.254.*' -and $_.InterfaceAlias -notlike 'vEthernet*' } | Select-Object -First 1).IPAddress
        $c = [Net.Sockets.TcpClient]::new()
        $ok = $false
        try { $ok = $c.ConnectAsync($ip, 47100).Wait(3000) -and $c.Connected } catch { $ok = $false } finally { $c.Dispose() }
        "connected: $ok"
    }

    Section "4b. (PSC-25/U0, repeated): psycopg implementation and libpq in the launched api (project $project)"
    Step "docker inspect $project-api-1 --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}}'" { docker inspect "$project-api-1" --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}}' }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api python -c `"$pyImpl`"" { docker compose exec -T api python -c $pyImpl }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api python -I -c <psycopg_c.pq origin; libpq files mapped in /proc/self/maps; psycopg_binary spec>' { docker compose exec -T api python -I -c $pyMaps }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api sh -c ''for f in /opt/venv/lib/python3*/site-packages/psycopg_c/pq*.so; do echo "$f"; ldd "$f" | grep -E "libpq|psycopg_binary"; done''' { docker compose exec -T api sh -c $shLdd }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api dpkg-query -W -f='`${Package} `${Version}\n' libpq5" { docker compose exec -T api dpkg-query -W -f='${Package} ${Version}\n' libpq5 }
    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyImpl`" (worker shares ke-app)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyImpl }

    Section "4c. (PSC-14, N10a/U1, repeated): no pip in the launched api; flattened ke-app (project $project)"
    Step "docker compose exec -T api python -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api python -c $pyPip }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c $pyPip }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api ls /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ (no pip wheel)' { docker compose exec -T api ls /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api ls -la /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ (same, long form)' { docker compose exec -T api ls -la /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ }
    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyPip`" (worker shares ke-app; must print None)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyPip }
    Step "docker image history ke-app:$tag" { docker image history "ke-app:$tag" }
    Step "docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' ke-app:$tag" { docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' "ke-app:$tag" }
    Step "docker image inspect ke-app:$tag --format '<RootFS layer count>; <Config.Env>'" { docker image inspect "ke-app:$tag" --format '{{len .RootFS.Layers}} rootfs layers; Env={{json .Config.Env}}' }

    Section '5. Safety shim verification (before the refusal case)'
    $shimLog = Join-Path $scratch 'shim.log'
    if (Test-Path $shimLog) { Remove-Item $shimLog }
    $env:KE_SHIM_LOG = $shimLog
    $realPath = $env:PATH
    $env:PATH = "$shimDir;$realPath"
    Step 'shim: Get-Command docker (resolves to the shim)' { (Get-Command docker).Source -replace [regex]::Escape($scratch), '<scratch>' }
    Step "shim: docker version --format '{{.Server.Version}}' (passes through)" { docker version --format '{{.Server.Version}}' }
    Step 'shim: docker compose up --help (must be intercepted, exit 99; harmless if not)' { docker compose up --help | Select-Object -First 2 }
    Step 'shim call log' { Get-Content $shimLog }
    Remove-Item $shimLog

    Section '5a. Refusal: KE_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0 and KE_ENV=prod in .env (launch.ps1 under the shim)'
    (Get-Content .env) -replace '^KE_BIND_ADDRESS=.*$', 'KE_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0' -replace '^KE_ENV=.*$', 'KE_ENV=prod' | Set-Content .env
    Step 'diff .env.example .env' { EnvDiff }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 (under the shim)' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 }
    Step 'shim call log (no compose up)' { Get-Content $shimLog; ''; "compose up reached: $([bool](Get-Content $shimLog | Where-Object { $_ -match '\bcompose\b' -and $_ -match '\bup\b' }))" }
    $env:PATH = $realPath
    Remove-Item Env:KE_SHIM_LOG
    Step 'project ports (unchanged, 127.0.0.1 only)' { ProjectPorts }
    Step 'Restore .env from <scratch>\env.bak; diff .env.example .env' { Copy-Item $envBak .env -Force; EnvDiff }
}
'down' {

    Step "docker image rm ke-app:$tag ke-gateway:$tag ke-gates:$tag (untag only; tester-owned tags)" { docker image rm "ke-app:$tag" "ke-gateway:$tag" "ke-gates:$tag" }
    Step "docker image ls --filter reference=ke-*:$tag" { docker image ls --filter "reference=ke-*:$tag" }
    Step 'git status --short' { git status --short }
}

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/docker.cmd ====================
@pwsh -NoProfile -File "%~dp0shim.ps1" %*
@exit /b %ERRORLEVEL%

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/shim.ps1 ====================
# Safety shim (tester, U3 Windows proof; copied from the U2 shim): logs each docker call, refuses any `docker compose ... up` with exit 99, passes the rest to the real docker.exe.
if ($env:KE_SHIM_LOG) { Add-Content -Path $env:KE_SHIM_LOG -Value ($args -join ' ') }
if (($args -contains 'compose') -and ($args -contains 'up')) { Write-Output 'shim: intercepted docker compose up (exit 99)'; exit 99 }
& 'C:\Program Files\Docker\Docker\resources\bin\docker.exe' @args
exit $LASTEXITCODE

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/bin/jq ====================
#!/bin/sh
# tester wrapper: the winget jq.exe in binary mode (-b, LF output), its documented use under MSYS2/Git Bash
exec "<profile>/AppData/Local/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/jqlang.jq_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/jq.exe" -b "$@"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/bundle-u4.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Section 3b (tester, U4 Windows proof): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this host, to a scratch --out
# (the U3 section 3b script, project/tag changed), then the U4 additions: the per-image unlicensed report lines,
# the syft /tmp/cache warning, and the image-side drift check run the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it
# (pull the base by digest, docker save, base SBOM through docker-compose.sbom.yml, sbom_check.py drift in ke-app).
set -u
S=<scratch>
R=/c/repos/Knowledge-engine
SM="$(cygpath -m "$S")"; SW="$(cygpath -w "$S")"
export RED_S="$S" RED_SM="$SM" RED_SW="$SW"
red() { perl -pe 's/\Q$ENV{RED_S}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SM}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SW}\E/<scratch>/gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#C:\\Users\\adam#<profile>#gi'; }
run() { echo "\$ $1" | red; bash -c "$1" 2>&1 | red; echo "[exit code: ${PIPESTATUS[0]}]"; echo; }
sec() { echo; echo "==================== $1 ===================="; echo; }
cd "$R"
export COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u4win KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4win-464fa11
export PATH="$S/bin:$PATH"
OUT="$S/bundle-u4win-amd64"
SHA12="$(git rev-parse HEAD | cut -c1-12)"
RAW="$S/out-u4/03b-bundle-raw.txt"
export S R OUT RAW

sec "3b. (PSC-13/U3, repeated; U4 additions: unlicensed report, /tmp/cache warning, image-side drift check): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this Windows host, to a scratch --out"
echo "date (UTC): $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
echo "COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME KE_IMAGE_TAG=$KE_IMAGE_TAG (exported, as build.ps1 tagged the images in phase build); --out <scratch>/bundle-u4win-amd64"
echo
run 'bash --version | head -1; uname -s -r; command -v cygpath docker'
echo '--- jq: bundle.sh needs it on PATH; the host has jq 1.8.2 from winget, not on the Git Bash PATH, and native jq.exe writes CRLF:'
run 'J=<profile>/AppData/Local/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/jqlang.jq_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/jq.exe; "$J" --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | "$J" -er .a | od -c | head -1; sha256sum "$J"'
echo '--- so <scratch>/bin/jq (on PATH for this section only) runs it with -b, LF output (as in U3):'
run 'cat "$(command -v jq)"; command -v jq; jq --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | jq -er .a | od -c | head -1'
run 'echo "id -u:id -g = $(id -u):$(id -g) (the KE_SBOM_USER bundle.sh passes)"'

echo '--- fail-closed from Git Bash (U3 L-2, repeated): KE_SBOM_USER empty, and unset; then set, with XDG_CACHE_HOME in the resolved environment (U4)'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER= docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'env -u KE_SBOM_USER KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --format json | jq -c ".services.sbom | {user, tmpfs, XDG_CACHE_HOME: .environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME, TMPDIR: .environment.TMPDIR}"'

echo '--- before'
run "ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"  # must not exist"
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l; ls -d "$S"/.ke-sbom.* "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # work dirs before: the U3 location (TMPDIR), then the U4 one beside --out (review N6) plus drift work dirs'
run 'docker compose -f docker-compose.yml --env-file .env.example config --format json | jq -r ".services|to_entries[]|\"\(.key) \(.value.image)\"" | sort  # the refs bundle.sh will save'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}} {{.Id}}' ke-app:u4win-464fa11 ke-gateway:u4win-464fa11"
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/|^anchore/syft' | sort  # tags bundle.sh (re)points; restored after if moved"
docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/out-u4/tags-before.txt"
run 'docker ps -a --format "{{.Names}} {{.Label \"com.docker.compose.project\"}}" | grep -c "ke-tester-u4win"  # containers of ke-tester-u4win* projects (expect 0: stack not up yet)'

echo '--- freshness (security M-1): bundle.sh uses KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4win-464fa11, the tag phase build just built from this tree'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} id={{.Id}} created={{.Created}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}' ke-app:u4win-464fa11 ke-gateway:u4win-464fa11"
APPID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-app:u4win-464fa11)"; GWID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-gateway:u4win-464fa11)"
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E ' ($APPID|$GWID)\$'  # every tag on the two fresh image IDs"
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win-probe --entrypoint python ke-app:u4win-464fa11 -I -c \"import importlib.util as u; print('psycopg_binary spec:', u.find_spec('psycopg_binary')); print('pip spec:', u.find_spec('pip')); import psycopg; print('psycopg impl:', psycopg.pq.__impl__, psycopg.pq.version())\""
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win-probe --entrypoint sh ke-app:u4win-464fa11 -c 'dpkg-query -W libpq5; ls -d /opt/venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/psycopg_binary* 2>&1 | head -3'"

echo '--- the run (full output; time in seconds after it; a raw copy is kept for the greps below)'
echo "\$ scripts/bundle.sh --out <scratch>/bundle-u4win-amd64"
t0=$(date +%s)
scripts/bundle.sh --out "$OUT" 2>&1 | red | tee "$RAW"
rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
echo "[exit code: $rc] [elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"
echo

echo '--- U4: the per-image unlicensed report lines bundle.sh printed (sbom_check.py finish, stderr), in run order'
run 'grep "^sbom_check: " "$RAW"'
run 'grep -E "^sbom_check: [a-z0-9-]+: unlicensed packages" "$RAW"  # the six header lines'
echo '--- U4: syft cache warnings in the bundle.sh run (ruling 5 item 6: XDG_CACHE_HOME=/tmp/cache)'
run 'grep -c "WARN" "$RAW"; grep -c "/tmp/cache" "$RAW"; grep -c "/.cache/syft" "$RAW"  # all WARN lines; those naming /tmp/cache; those naming the U3 path /.cache/syft'
run 'grep -m1 "/tmp/cache" "$RAW"  # one occurrence'
run 'grep "WARN" "$RAW" | sort | uniq -c  # every distinct WARN line'

echo '--- the six SBOMs (and the image tars they describe)'
run "cd \"$OUT\" && ls -la app/sboms && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' app/sboms/* && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' images/*"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && cat bundle.json"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && grep -c '' MANIFEST.sha256 && grep 'app/sboms/' MANIFEST.sha256"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && sha256sum -c --quiet MANIFEST.sha256 && echo 'MANIFEST.sha256: every file verifies'"
run "cat \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; (cd \"$S\" && sha256sum bundle-u4win-amd64/MANIFEST.sha256)"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/check_sboms.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R")\"  # validate() per file, the tests' equivalent (U3 helper, unchanged)"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/recount_unlicensed.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/scripts/sbom_check.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT")\"  # host recount with sbom_check.unlicensed_report on the written files (must equal the printed counts)"

sec "3b-drift. U4 (ruling 6; PSC-14/22, N7, N10 B3): the image-side drift check against this run's ke-app SBOM, the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it"
echo "Commands are offline_check.sh's own (host half, after bundle.sh), with its variables set the same way; the one difference: the drift work dir is"
echo "made in <scratch> (mktemp -d <scratch>/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX) rather than the repo's dist/, so this run writes nothing in the repo. The base pull"
echo "output is shown here (offline_check.sh sends its stdout to /dev/null). The SBOM run inherits COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME (ke-tester-u4win), as"
echo "offline_check's inherits ke-offline-<random> (ruling 7)."
echo
run 'grep -n -E "^FROM .* AS runtime$|^ARG LIBPQ_VERSION=" docker/app.Dockerfile'
base_ref="$(sed -n 's/^FROM \([^ ]*\) AS runtime$/\1/p' docker/app.Dockerfile)"
libpq_pin="$(sed -n 's/^ARG LIBPQ_VERSION=//p' docker/app.Dockerfile)"
app_ref="$(sed -n 's/.*"ref": "\(ke-app:[^"]*\)".*/\1/p' "$OUT/bundle.json" | head -n 1)"
export base_ref libpq_pin app_ref
run '[[ "$base_ref" =~ ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._/-]*:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$ ]] && echo "base_ref=$base_ref (passes the offline_check.sh pin regex)"; echo "libpq_pin=$libpq_pin"; echo "app_ref=$app_ref (from bundle.json)"'
echo '--- the base in the image store before the pull (present: an earlier offline_check run pulled it; not removed here, a shared image)'
run 'docker image ls --digests --no-trunc --format "{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.Digest}} {{.ID}} {{.CreatedSince}}" python | grep "${base_ref##*@}"; docker image inspect --format "{{.Id}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}" "$base_ref"'
echo '--- pull by digest (offline_check: docker pull "$base_ref" > /dev/null || die ...; one call, no retry)'
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker pull "$base_ref"; rc=$?; echo "[pull elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; exit $rc'
DW="$(mktemp -d "$S/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX")"; export DW
run 'echo "drift_work=$DW"; mkdir -- "$DW/in" "$DW/out" "$DW/out/tmp" && chmod 0700 -- "$DW/in" "$DW/out" "$DW/out/tmp" && ls -la "$DW" "$DW/out"'
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker save -o "$DW/in/base.tar" "$base_ref"; rc=$?; echo "[save elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$DW/in/base.tar"; exit $rc'
echo '--- the base SBOM (offline_check.sh lines, verbatim; hostpath = cygpath -m); its WARN lines are part of this output'
run 'hostpath() { if command -v cygpath > /dev/null 2>&1; then cygpath -m -- "$1"; else printf "%s" "$1"; fi; }; t0=$(date +%s); MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 KE_SBOM_IN="$(hostpath "$DW/in")" KE_SBOM_OUT="$(hostpath "$DW/out")" KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml run --rm --pull never sbom docker-archive:/in/base.tar --scope all-layers -o spdx-json=/out/raw.spdx.json >&2; rc=$?; echo "[sbom elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; exit $rc'
run 'stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json"; jq -r "\"spdxVersion=\(.spdxVersion) name=\(.name) packages=\(.packages|length)\"" "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json"'
echo '--- sbom_check.py drift in the ke-app image (offline_check.sh lines, verbatim)'
DRIFT='hostpath() { if command -v cygpath > /dev/null 2>&1; then cygpath -m -- "$1"; else printf "%s" "$1"; fi; }; repo_host="$(hostpath "$PWD")"; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --read-only --cap-drop ALL --security-opt no-new-privileges --pull never --user "$(id -u):$(id -g)" -v "$repo_host/scripts/sbom_check.py:/opt/ke/sbom_check.py:ro" -v "$repo_host/tests/test_notice_ke_app.py:/opt/ke/added.py:ro" -v "$repo_host/licenses:/opt/ke/licenses:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$OUT/app/sboms"):/sboms:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$DW/out"):/base:ro" --entrypoint python "$app_ref" -I /opt/ke/sbom_check.py drift'
LIC='--licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-incorporated.txt --licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-HACL.txt --licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-KaRaMeL.txt'
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /base/raw.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq \"\$libpq_pin\" --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC && echo \"offline: image-side drift check: the ke-app SBOM matches its base (\$base_ref)\""
echo '--- what the checker compared (tester, host side, sbom_check.py loaded by path; informational)'
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/drift_inputs.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/scripts/sbom_check.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT/app/sboms/ke-app.spdx.json")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/tests/test_notice_ke_app.py")\""
echo '--- negative controls (tester): the same docker run, one input wrong each; both must exit 1 naming the problem'
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /base/raw.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq 15.18-0+deb12u1 --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC  # control 1: wrong libpq pin"
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq \"\$libpq_pin\" --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC  # control 2: the ke-app SBOM as its own base (adds no Debian package)"
echo '--- drift cleanup (offline_check: rm -rf -- "$drift_work")'
run 'rm -rf -- "$DW"; ls -d "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l'

echo '--- after: nothing left by the run'
run 'docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win-sbom -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win-sbomcheck -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win-notices -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4win -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter ancestor=ke-app:u4win-464fa11 -q | wc -l  # sbom, sbomcheck, notices; the drift SBOM run (project ke-tester-u4win); the drift checker (no label: ancestor ke-app)'
run 'docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.service=sbom | wc -l  # sbom containers, any project'
run 'docker compose ls -a --filter name=ke-tester-u4win'
run 'docker network ls --filter name=ke-tester-u4win'
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l; ls -d "$S"/.ke-sbom.* "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # work dirs left (trap removes; expect the counts from before)'
docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/out-u4/tags-after.txt"
run "diff \"$S/out-u4/tags-before.txt\" \"$S/out-u4/tags-after.txt\" && echo 'bundle and ke-vendor tags: unchanged'"
if ! diff -q "$S/out-u4/tags-before.txt" "$S/out-u4/tags-after.txt" > /dev/null; then
  echo '--- restoring moved tags to their earlier image IDs'
  while read -r t id; do run "docker tag $id $t"; done < "$S/out-u4/tags-before.txt"
  comm -13 "$S/out-u4/tags-before.txt" "$S/out-u4/tags-after.txt" | while read -r t id; do grep -q "^$t " "$S/out-u4/tags-before.txt" || run "docker image rm $t  # new tag from this run"; done
  run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/' | sort | diff \"$S/out-u4/tags-before.txt\" - && echo 'tags restored'"
fi

echo '--- keep the six SBOMs and bundle.json in the tester scratch dir <scratch>/u4win-sboms, then delete the scratch bundle'
run "rm -rf \"$S/u4win-sboms\" && mkdir \"$S/u4win-sboms\" && cp -- \"$OUT\"/app/sboms/*.spdx.json \"$OUT/bundle.json\" \"$S/u4win-sboms/\" && (cd \"$S/u4win-sboms\" && sha256sum *.spdx.json)"
run "rm -rf -- \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\""
echo "[end 3b]"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/recount_unlicensed.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4 Windows proof): recounts the unlicensed report of each SBOM a real bundle.sh run wrote,
with scripts/sbom_check.py loaded by path (never imported from the repo's package path). argv: <sbom_check.py> <bundle dir>."""
import importlib.util, json, pathlib, sys

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ke_sbom_check_under_test", sys.argv[1])
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
out = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
for name in ("ke-app", "ke-gateway", "neo4j", "phoenix", "postgres", "valkey"):
    doc = json.loads((out / "app" / "sboms" / f"{name}.spdx.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    r = m.unlicensed_report(doc)
    print(f"{name}: {r['count']} {dict(sorted(r['by_type'].items()))}")

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/drift_inputs.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4 Windows proof, informational): what `sbom_check.py drift` compares, recomputed on the host with
scripts/sbom_check.py loaded by path. argv: <sbom_check.py> <ke-app SBOM> <base SBOM> <tests/test_notice_ke_app.py>."""
import importlib.util, json, sys

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ke_sbom_check_under_test", sys.argv[1])
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
a = json.load(open(sys.argv[2], encoding="utf-8"))
b = json.load(open(sys.argv[3], encoding="utf-8"))
added = m.added_packages(open(sys.argv[4], encoding="utf-8").read())
d = sorted(set(m._named(a, "deb")) - set(m._named(b, "deb")))
print("Debian packages ke-app adds to its base:", d)
print("ADDED_PACKAGES (tests/test_notice_ke_app.py, by ast):", sorted(added))
print("equal:", set(d) == set(added))
print("base SBOM: deb", len(m._named(b, "deb")), "pypi", len(m._named(b, "pypi")), "generic", sorted(m._named(b, "generic")))
print("ke-app libpq5:", sorted(m._named(a, "deb").get("libpq5", [])))
print("ke-app psycopg-c:", sorted(m._named(a, "pypi").get("psycopg-c", [])), "psycopg-binary present:", "psycopg-binary" in m._named(a, "pypi"))
print("ke-app pip present:", "pip" in m._named(a, "pypi"))
print("ke-app CPython (pkg:generic/python):", sorted(m._named(a, "generic").get("python", [])))
